Use when the user asks to mine a transcript, find skill opportunities, reduce approval prompts, identify recurring automation, or asks what skills they should create. Also use when the user explicitly says they repeatedly perform or approve the same workflow. ([klgraham/agent_skills](https://github.com/klgraham/agent_skills/tree/HEAD/skills/transcript-skill-miner))
- **transcript-splitter**: Split a cleaned RPG session source into ordered story beats while preserving full source coverage. Use when producing `beats.json` from a cleaned transcript or a normalized non-transcript source, especially when beat titles, date inference, time windows, combat sub-splits, and markdown preview artifacts are needed. ([tsackton/taelgar](https://github.com/tsackton/taelgar/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/transcript-splitter))
- **transcript-summarize**: Downloads video transcripts via subtitles using yt-dlp, deduplicates and cleans them into plain-text files, then produces an AI summary of each video. WHEN: "summarize video", "video summary", "transcript summary", "summarize YouTube", "get transcript", "video transcript", "watch summary". ([theontho/skill-dump](https://github.com/theontho/skill-dump/tree/HEAD/skills/transcript-summarize))
- **transcript-summarizer**: Summarize meeting transcripts into structured notes with decisions, action items, and key topics. ([mindmorass/reflex](https://github.com/mindmorass/reflex/tree/HEAD/plugins/reflex/skills/transcript-summarizer) · ★2)
- **transcript-summary**: Turn a transcript into a summary that leads with analysis and preserves every decision, action item, open question, and nuance the recording carried ([BadGuyFranco/CoProducer](https://github.com/BadGuyFranco/CoProducer/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/transcript-summary))
- **transcript-summary**: 把转录内容整理成商务/投资会议的核心观点总结：按发言人分组（发言人标题用 H1），每条观点提炼一句话结论（加粗）+论述，标题标出项目或主讲人，纠正专业词汇的转写错误，过滤寒暄，财务数字零容忍编造。当用户说"把这份转录整理成核心观点总结""导出一份 summary 文档""帮我提炼这次会议的要点""按发言人整理观点""这是投资人/创始人的录音，整理成决策参考"时使用。典型输入是 transcript-md 产出的结构化 Markdown（带说话人加粗标注、章节），拿到原始转录稿或其他格式也直接处理，不强制先跑一遍 `transcript-md`。注意与 `gamma-ppt-outline` 区分：那个产出 PPT 页面结构且不分发言人；本 skill 产出按发言人分组的观点提炼文档，且允许改写措辞、合并归纳（不是逐字保真）。 ([GarageDev1/alex-skill](https://github.com/GarageDev1/alex-skill/tree/HEAD/transcript-summary))
